# Level, objects, and gameplay

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Status**: Supported<br>
> **Minimum version**: 1.4

## Overview

This section lists level, object, action, equipment, and gameplay event registrations. An object method may require a particular subtype.

## Syntax

| Call | Arguments and result |
| --- | --- |
| `sim.net_find(id)` | Looks up an already registered level object by numeric ID. Returns a `game_object` or `nil`. |
| `obj:cast_Car()` | Returns a `CCar` for a car, or `nil`. |
| `car:add_fuel(amount)` | Adds a positive amount of fuel up to tank capacity and returns the amount actually added. |
| `obj:get_bone_id_by_name(name)` | Returns a bone ID or `65535`. Check the result before using it as an ID. |
| `obj:bone_direction(id_or_name)` | Returns a world-space `vector` for the bone’s local Z axis. The object needs a skeletal model. |
| `obj:cast_Artefact()` | Returns a `CArtefact` or `nil`. |
| `artefact:GetInventoryWeightModifier()` | Returns the current numeric carry-weight modifier of this artefact. |
| `artefact:SetInventoryWeightModifier(value)` | Sets the numeric modifier on this instance. |
| `console:register_lua_command(name, handler, tips)` | Registers a uniquely named Lua command. The handler receives a 1-indexed table of strings. `tips` is a comma-separated list of suggestions. |

## Examples

### Refuel a car by ID

Pass the ID of a car on the loaded level and the amount of fuel. The function returns `0` if that car is unavailable.

```lua
local function refuel_car(id, amount)
    local object = sim.net_find(id)
    local car = object and object:cast_Car()
    if not car or amount <= 0 then return 0 end
    return car:add_fuel(amount)
end
```

### Read a bone direction

Pass an object with a skeletal model and a bone name. The direction can be used to orient an effect.

```lua
local function bone_forward(object, name)
    if not object then return nil end
    local id = object:get_bone_id_by_name(name)
    if id == 65535 then return nil end
    return object:bone_direction(id)
end
```

### Read an artefact modifier

The cast handles an inventory item or an object from the level.

```lua
local function get_artefact_weight_bonus(object)
    local artefact = object and object:cast_Artefact()
    if not artefact then return nil end
    return artefact:GetInventoryWeightModifier()
end
```

### Add a mod console command

Call `register_commands()` when initializing the mod in the Lua VM. Then enter `my_mod_echo hello` in the console to print `hello`.

```lua
local function register_commands()
    local console = get_console()
    console:register_lua_command("my_mod_echo", function(args)
        printf("%s", tostring(args[1] or ""))
    end, "message")
end
```

| `FFxRandom:next_bool()` | Method | Advances to the next value. Result `boolean`. Implementation `return next_float() < 0.5;`. | [level_script.cpp:1844](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L1844) |
| `FFxRandom:next_bool_probability(probability)` | Method | Advances to the next value. Arguments `probability` (`number`). Result `boolean`. Implementation `return next_float() < probability;`. | [level_script.cpp:1845](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L1845) |
| `FFxRandom:next_float()` | Method | Advances to the next value. Result `number`. Implementation `return static_cast<float>(next_int()) * inv_2pow31;`. | [level_script.cpp:1842](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L1842) |
| `FFxRandom:next_float_range(min, max)` | Method | Advances to the next value. Arguments `min` (`number`), `max` (`number`). Result `number`. Implementation `if (min > max) std::swap(min, max); return min + next_float() * (max - min);`. | [level_script.cpp:1843](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L1843) |
| `FFxRandom:next_int()` | Method | Advances to the next value. Result `number`. | [level_script.cpp:1840](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L1840) |
| `FFxRandom:next_int_range(min, max)` | Method | Advances to the next value. Arguments `min` (`number`), `max` (`number`). Result `number`. | [level_script.cpp:1841](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L1841) |
| `FFxRandom:set_state(seed, counter)` | Method | Sets the state. Arguments `seed` (`number`), `counter` (`number`). | [level_script.cpp:1839](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L1839) |

### `level`

| Call or name | Kind | Description | Registration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `level.actor_in_restrictor(name)` | Function | Returns the actor in restrictor. Arguments `name` (`string`). Result `boolean`. | [level_script.cpp:2036](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L2036) |
| `level.block_action(cmd)` | Function | Blocks the action. Arguments `cmd` (`number`). Implementation `if (g_pGameLevel == nullptr) { return; } Level().block_action(cmd);`. | [level_script.cpp:2049](https://github.com/ixray-team/ixray-1.6-stcop/blob/aa90b5cceb412f52b0108150b20b7343f5cd487b/src/xrGame/level_script.cpp#L2049) |
